Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in precipitation theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 956 and the invariant I(A,B) = 441 mod 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to precipitation theory, we decompose the problem into 956 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 441. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
